Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on video generation backbones are a rising recipe for robot learning, yet remain confined to tabletop manipulation. Mobile manipulation demands simultaneous locomotion and whole-body manipulation amid scene-scale dynamics, yet is still dominated by dynamics-blind visual encoders with hand-crafted coordination. We bridge this gap with MobileWAM, a mixture-of-transformers architecture that fuses a pretrained video diffusion transformer with a lightweight action expert through layerwise joint attention, translating internet-scale motion priors into whole-body control. To reconcile the heterogeneous dynamics of moving and manipulating, each feed-forward layer of the action expert becomes a three-expert mixture of shared, locomotion, and manipulation experts, softly routed by the motion intent in the action tokens. To densify supervision, we further propose Chain-of-Foresight (CoF): intermediate representations sequentially predict a chain of future latent chunks, each step conditioned on its predecessor. CoF pairs naturally with our decoupled video--action denoising scheme. At deployment, the WAM serves as a pure current-frame encoder; foresight acts only through gradients, so at inference the foresight chain and video generation are discarded, leaving only policy-level cost. MobileWAM surpasses state-of-the-art mobile manipulation policies on ManiSkill-HAB and fine-tunes to a real ARX Lift2 mobile manipulator across diverse tasks with strong generalization. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [66] [VQ-VAD: Vector-quantized Motion Representation Learning for Human-centric Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05069)
*Narges Rashvand,Ghazal Alinezhad Noghre,Shanle Yao,Gabriel Maldonado,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VQ-VAD，一种基于姿态的视频异常检测框架，利用VQ-GAN的向量量化思想对关键点序列学习离散运动表示，通过重建误差检测异常。在四个基准上验证了域内、跨域和跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于姿态的VAD方法在连续潜在空间中建模人类行为，难以学习紧凑的运动模式，限制了行为分析的鲁棒性。同时，直接使用原始视频存在视觉噪声和隐私问题。

Method: VQ-VAD将原本用于图像生成的VQ-GAN适配到关键点序列上，构建正常行为的运动码本（motion codebook），仅在正常运动序列上训练。检测时将观测运动序列映射到码本，通过高重建误差识别异常。

Result: 在HR-SHT上达到81.83%的域内准确率；从CMU Panoptic无需微调跨域迁移到HR-SHT达到76.69%；在四个异常检测基准上展示了有竞争力的跨数据集鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VQ-VAD证明离散运动表示比连续潜在空间更适合姿态类视频异常检测，在隐私保护和跨域泛化方面表现优异，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) is inherently challenging due to the scarcity of anomalies and the large visual variability in surveillance footage, including changes in lighting, viewpoint, and human appearance. To mitigate visual noise and address privacy concerns, recent work has shifted to pose-based VAD, which focuses on motion dynamics rather than raw video data. However, existing pose-based approaches model human behavior in continuous latent spaces, limiting their ability to learn compact motion patterns necessary for robust behavior analysis. We address this by proposing Vector-Quantized Video Anomaly Detection (VQ-VAD), a novel human-centric anomaly detection framework that learns discrete motion representations. VQ-VAD adapts Vector-Quantized GAN (VQ-GAN), originally developed for image generation, to operate on keypoint sequences and construct a motion codebook of normal behavior. Trained exclusively on normal motion sequences, VQ-VAD detects anomalies by identifying high reconstruction errors when an observed motion sequence cannot be mapped to the learned codebook. We conduct extensive experiments across three complementary evaluation settings, including in-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset generalization, on four anomaly detection benchmarks. VQ-VAD achieves strong in-domain accuracy (81.83% on HR-SHT [15]), effective cross-domain transfer from CMU Panoptic [14] (76.69% on HR-SHT [15] without retraining), and competitive cross-dataset robustness. The code base for this work is available at https://github.com/TeCSAR-UNCC/VQ-VAD.

</details>


### [67] [Differential 6-DOF Pose Estimation with Provable First-Order Immunity to Camera Calibration Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04673)
*Yueqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Yi Zhang,Baoqiong Wang,Wenjun Chen,Shuixin Pan,Yulan Guo,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种差分位姿估计方法，通过帧间图像位移和已知3D控制点直接恢复平台运动，避免了传统3D-2D方法对绝对位姿估计和相机外参标定的依赖，在6自由度平台微运动估计中达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-2D方法在每个时刻独立估计绝对相机位姿，再通过相机到平台的外参恢复平台运动，这对外参标定误差极为敏感，尤其是在微小运动场景下。需要一种直接恢复平台运动、绕过绝对位姿估计和标定误差影响的方法。

Method: 通过对透视投影方程做差分，结合深度不变性近似，并在SE(3)上建模运动。理论上证明平移外参误差完全抵消，旋转外参误差仅引起由标定误差、运动幅度和观测几何决定的有界扰动。推导了通用可观测性条件、Cramer-Rao下界及偏差消除的一致估计器，并刻画了近似的有效极限。支持单目和多相机系统。

Result: 广泛的合成与真实实验表明该方法在6-DOF平台微运动估计中达到最先进水平。在5个控制点、0.5像素图像噪声下，单目求解器获得旋转RMSE为10.09角秒、平移RMSE为3.70 mm、运行时间0.34 ms；双目求解器旋转RMSE为10.58角秒、平移RMSE为3.91 mm、运行时间0.27 ms。在精度、标定鲁棒性和计算效率上优于代表性PnP和广义PnP方法。

Conclusion: 差分位姿估计方法通过避免独立绝对位姿估计，从根本上提升了微运动估计对标定误差的鲁棒性，兼具高精度和高计算效率，为机器人操作、自主系统和结构位移监测等应用提供了新的最优方案。

Abstract: Accurate six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) motion estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, autonomous systems, and structural displacement monitoring. Conventional 3D-2D methods estimate absolute camera poses independently at each time and recover platform motion through camera-to-platform extrinsics, making them sensitive to extrinsic calibration errors, especially for micromotion. We present a differential pose estimation method that directly recovers platform motion from inter-frame image displacements and known 3D control points. By differencing perspective projection equations, using a depth-invariance approximation, and modeling motion on SE(3), the method avoids independent absolute-pose estimation and supports both monocular and multi-camera systems. We prove that translational extrinsic errors cancel exactly, while rotational errors induce a bounded perturbation determined by calibration error, motion magnitude, and observation geometry. We also derive generic observability conditions, a Cramer-Rao lower bound, and a bias-eliminated consistent estimator, and characterize the validity limits of the approximations. Extensive synthetic and real-world experiments establish a new state of the art for 6-DOF platform micromotion estimation, outperforming representative PnP and generalized-PnP methods in accuracy, calibration robustness, and computational efficiency. With five control points and 0.5-pixel image noise, the monocular solver obtains a combined pitch-yaw rotation RMSE of 10.09 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.70 mm, and a runtime of 0.34 ms. The binocular solver achieves a rotation RMSE of 10.58 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.91 mm, and a runtime of 0.27 ms. Code will be released upon publication at https://github.com/zyoungszu/pami2026.

</details>


### [68] [Robust and Efficient Motion Reasoning for Privacy-Aware Classroom Incident Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05115)
*Paritosh Parmar,Landy Lan,Hong Yang,Chen Yi,Chiat Pin Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种隐私感知且计算高效的教室事件识别框架，通过层次运动学表示与知识蒸馏（将大模型的多阶运动推理压缩为轻量单阶学生模型），在CCTV风格混合基准上以低于基线十分之一的计算成本实现更优性能，并具备出色的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教室安全事件识别在实际部署中面临隐私、计算效率和泛化三大挑战，而现有研究缺乏专门为此设计的基准和方法。大多数教室事件在运动方向、速度、加速度和强度上差异显著，而非仅姿态不同，现有基于姿态的方法未能充分利用运动学信息。

Method: 1) 构建融合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实教室姿态数据的混合基准；2) 提出轻量级运动推理框架：首先构建人类动作的层次运动学表示（方向、速度、加速度、强度），然后通过知识蒸馏将大规模教师模型的多阶运动推理能力压缩到单阶学生模型中，实现高效逐人推理。

Result: 实验表明，该模型以低于基线十分之一的计算成本取得了更优性能，同时在跨域运动推理和合成数据到真实数据的零样本泛化方面表现更强。基准、代码和工具将公开发布。

Conclusion: 层次运动学表示结合知识蒸馏是平衡教室安全场景中隐私、效率与性能的有效方案，为隐私感知教室安全研究提供了新基准与可行方向。

Abstract: Can computer vision help make classrooms safer? In this pilot study, we investigate privacy-aware and computationally efficient classroom incident recognition from CCTV-style observations. This setting remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and few methods designed for the privacy, efficiency, and generalization demands of real-world deployment. We introduce a novel hybrid benchmark combining generative CCTV-style videos with real-world classroom pose data, and propose a lightweight, but robust motion-reasoning framework motivated by the observation that many incidents differ more in motion direction, speed, acceleration, and intensity than in pose alone. To that end, our method first constructs hierarchical kinematic representations of human actions. Our method then distills hierarchical, multi-order kinematic reasoning from a large teacher into a much smaller single-order student, enabling efficient per-person inference while preserving expressive motion understanding. Experiments show that our model outperforms substantially larger baselines at less than one-tenth of their computational cost, while also demonstrating stronger out-of-domain motion reasoning and zero-shot synthetic-to-real generalization. We will publicly release the benchmark, codebase, and supporting tools to facilitate further research in privacy-aware classroom safety.

</details>


### [69] [SurgNarrator: A Generative Retrieval Framework for Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04676)
*Yuqing Feng,Jiawei Ma,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Kun Yuan,Nicolas Padoy,Daniel S. Elson,Anh Nguyen,Stamatia Giannarou,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgNarrator，一种针对手术视频理解的生成式检索框架，通过手术专属词汇空间、时序感知对比学习和分层检索策略，在零样本设置下实现低延迟且高精度的手术视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法面临两难：自回归视频语言模型推理能力强但延迟高，不适合临床时间敏感场景；对比模型延迟低但复杂场景理解能力弱。生成式检索虽在通用领域有探索，但直接迁移到手术领域存在困难，因为外科场景中视觉外观相近可能对应不同语义事件，且术语具有高度专业性。

Method: 1) 从手术字幕中构建精心策划的手术特异性词汇库，定义临床有意义的检索空间；2) 适配预训练的Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B模型，使用时序感知对比目标学习判别性临床表征；3) 推理阶段采用分层、手术流程感知的检索策略，将搜索空间缩小到相关手术类型，实现快速有效的响应。

Result: 在十二个基准的零样本设置下进行综合评估，一致性优于最先进的基线方法，同时相比生成式基线将输出阶段延迟降低了两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SurgNarrator通过生成式检索框架有效解决了手术视频理解中准确性与延迟的权衡问题，为时间敏感的临床决策支持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Surgical procedures unfold as structured and recurring clinical events, whose real-time understanding via intraoperative surgical videos is critical for intraoperative decision-making and support. However, existing video understanding methods force a trade-off: autoregressive video-language models support comprehensive reasoning but are not practical for time-sensitive clinical applications, whereas contrastive models offer low latency but struggle with complex scene understanding. Recently, generative retrieval has been explored for general-domain video understanding, but transferring it to surgery is not trivial because near-identical visual appearances may indicate semantically distinct events, and the terminology involved is highly surgery-specific. To this end, we propose SurgNarrator, a new generative retrieval framework tailored for surgical video understanding. We construct a well-curated surgery-centric vocabulary from surgical captions to define a clinically meaningful retrieval space. We then adapt the pre-trained Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to learn discriminative clinical representations with a temporally-aware contrastive objective. During inference, a hierarchical, procedure-aware retrieval strategy narrows the search space to the relevant procedure type, delivering fast and effective responses. Our method is comprehensively evaluated on twelve benchmarks in a zero-shot setting and achieves consistent performance gains over state-of-the-art baselines, while reducing output-stage latency by more than two orders of magnitude compared with the generative baseline.

</details>


### [70] [OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131)
*Aniri,Jinhe Bi,Peng Liao,Zengjie Jin,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPD-V，一种基于视觉的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）范式，通过正负教师对暴露并利用模态平衡信息作为特权信息，以解决多模态大语言模型推理中的模态不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OPSD方法从多样输入源提取特权信息来引导自蒸馏，但忽略了多模态大语言模型推理中固有的模态不平衡挑战——当文本信息主导生成时，模型无法充分整合多模态输入，导致精心设计的特权信息未被充分利用，限制了OPSD的效果。

Method: 通过构建正教师（Zoom-In图像）和负教师（Mask图像），它们展现出不同程度的模态不平衡，并观察到模态平衡本身可以作为特权信息。基于此发现，引入OPD-V范式：使用正模态平衡对数间隔定义模态平衡信任区域，该区域选择用于自蒸馏的在线策略token。

Result: 在6个基准、4种多模态大语言模型骨干和5种后训练方法上进行实验，OPD-V一致地提升了推理性能，同时降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: 模态平衡可以作为特权信息指导视觉OPSD，且通过信任区域选择在线策略token的方式能有效提升多模态推理性能并减少训练开销。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has become a standard post-training approach for improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing methods draw privileged information from diverse input sources to guide self-distillation. Yet these designs overlook Modality Imbalance, a challenge inherent to MLLM reasoning. When textual information dominates generation, the model cannot fully integrate its multimodal input. Consequently, carefully designed privileged information remains underused, limiting the effectiveness of OPSD. To examine this limitation, we construct a Positive Teacher with the Zoom-In Image and a Negative Teacher with the Mask Image, which exhibit different degrees of Modality Imbalance. Changes in their reasoning correctness and token logits reveal that Modality Balance can itself serve as privileged information. Motivated by this finding, we introduce OPD-V, a visual OPSD paradigm that instantiates such information through the Positive Teacher and Negative Teacher. Positive Modality-Balance Logits Margins define a Modality-Balance Trust Region that selects the on-policy tokens used for self-distillation. Experiments across 6 benchmarks, 4 MLLM backbones, and 5 post-training methods show that OPD-V consistently improves reasoning performance while reducing training cost.

</details>


### [71] [UniWorld-View: Large-Baseline View Synthesis via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04701)
*Haiyang Zhou,Wangbo Yu,Chaoran Feng,Xunyu Zhou,Yonghong Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-View是一个统一的框架，将显式3D几何引导与生成式扩散建模相结合，用于从单目输入进行可控的大基线新视角合成。通过遮挡感知点云渲染策略提供精确的几何先验，并利用视频扩散骨干网络实现高保真、几何一致的新视角生成，支持极端相机运动和多视角视频输出，适用于动态3DGS重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上大量单目视频和图像为沉浸式内容创作提供了宝贵素材，但从稀疏观测生成新视角极具挑战性。基于重建的方法（如NeRF和3DGS）在稀疏输入下性能严重恶化且无法处理遮挡；生成式方法虽降低数据需求，但受限于不精确或隐式的几何引导，难以处理大基线视角合成。现有方法在可控性、几何一致性和视觉保真度方面均存在不足。

Method: UniWorld-View提出一个统一框架，将显式3D几何引导与生成式扩散建模相结合。其核心创新包括：(1) 遮挡感知点云渲染策略，通过解决可见性歧义提供精确的几何先验用于扩散合成；(2) 将渲染策略与强大的视频扩散骨干网络耦合，实现精确相机控制和几何一致的视图生成；(3) 支持输出多视角视频，可用于下游动态3DGS重建。

Result: 在WorldScore基准和零样本NVS基准上的实验表明，UniWorld-View在可控性、几何一致性和视觉保真度方面均表现优异，能够处理极端相机运动和大基线变化下的高保真新视角生成。

Conclusion: UniWorld-View通过结合显式几何引导和生成式扩散建模，有效解决了稀疏单目输入下的大基线新视角合成难题，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性，为沉浸式内容创作提供了先进的解决方案。

Abstract: The abundance of casually captured monocular videos and images on social media provides a valuable source for immersive content creation, where generating novel views from such sparse observations can greatly enhance user experiences. However, producing photorealistic and geometrically consistent views with precise camera control remains challenging when input coverage is extremely limited. Reconstruction-based approaches such as NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) deteriorate severely under sparse inputs and fail to explicitly handle occlusions. Generative methods ease data requirements but still struggle with large-baseline view synthesis due to inaccurate or implicit geometric guidance. To overcome these limitations, we introduce UniWorld-View, a unified framework for controllable large-baseline novel view synthesis from monocular inputs. UniWorld-View integrates explicit 3D guidance with generative diffusion modeling to enable precise camera control and geometrically consistent view generation. The geometric guidance is obtained through an occlusion-aware point cloud rendering strategy that resolves visibility ambiguities and provides accurate priors for diffusion-based synthesis. By coupling this rendering strategy with powerful video diffusion backbones, UniWorld-View achieves high-fidelity novel view generation even under extreme camera motions and wide-baseline changes, and can further provide multi-view videos for downstream dynamic 3DGS reconstruction. Experiments on the WorldScore benchmark and zero-shot NVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of UniWorld-View in controllability, geometric consistency, and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [72] [A Multi-Sensor Dataset for Monitoring the Operational Environment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04704)
*Claudio Diotallevi,Rodrigo Gudiño,Zaharia Pachalieva,Philipp Neumaier,Patrick Naumann,Erik Bochinski,Volker Eiselein,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在德国数字化铁路（DSD）计划下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH联合开发的铁路环境感知多传感器数据集，包含超过700万个高质量标注，覆盖铁路专用和通用感知对象在不同运营场景下的数据，可用于训练和验证AI驱动的铁路环境监测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的铁路环境监测对自动化铁路系统（GoA2至GoA4）的安全高效运营至关重要，而AI感知系统的开发需要大量精确标注的数据进行训练和验证，但目前缺乏专门针对铁路环境感知需求的大规模多传感器数据集。

Method: 在Digitale Schiene Deutschland (DSD)计划框架下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH合作开发了一个综合性的多传感器数据集，涵盖铁路特定对象和通用感知对象，在多种运营场景下采集，包含超过700万个高质量标注。

Result: 该数据集已最终完成，包含超过700万个标注，覆盖铁路专用和通用感知对象，可在多种运营场景下获取，现已在DB InfraGO AG开放请求获取。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为推进铁路领域AI驱动环境监测的宝贵资源，为自动化铁路系统的AI感知系统开发和验证提供支撑。

Abstract: Reliable environment monitoring is essential for the safe and efficient operation of automated railway systems, covering all Grades of Automation (GoA), from partially automated (GoA2) to fully automated operation (GoA4). Artificial Intelligence (AI) plays a central role in enabling these systems to detect, classify, and react to potential hazards in real time. The development of such AI-based perception systems requires large volumes of accurately annotated data for training and validation.
  Within the Digitale Schiene Deutschland (DSD) program, DB InfraGO AG and understandAI GmbH have developed a comprehensive multi- sensor dataset tailored to the needs of railway environment perception. This dataset contains over 7 million high-quality annotations of both railway-specific and general perception objects, captured under varying operational scenarios. The finalized dataset can now be requested at the DB InfraGO AG and serve as a valuable resource for advancing AI-driven environment monitoring in the railway domain.

</details>


### [73] [YOLOv14:Unified Cross-Domain Real-Time Object Detectionwith Adaptive Multi-View Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04720)
*Jinling Jia,Jian Lu,Jone Yawl,Chenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLOv14是一个统一的目标检测框架，通过四项创新（可变形区域注意力、游戏到真实域自适应、多视角条件注入和自适应增强策略）应对鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染角色、航拍视角和360°全景等非理想输入，在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP，推理速度2.91ms（T4 GPU），并在各特殊基准上大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实时目标检测器在受控条件下表现优异，但在非理想输入（鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染角色、航拍视角、360°全景）上性能急剧下降，缺乏一个统一的框架来应对这些多样化场景。

Method: 提出了四项协同创新：(1) Deformable Area-Attention (D-AAttn)，用学习的2D形变场替代刚性注意力网格，在几何畸变下自适应采样；(2) Game2Real域自适应，通过AdaIN和对抗域混淆对齐游戏与真实图像特征分布；(3) 多视角条件注入，在骨干网络中注入学习到的视角嵌入，并通过跨视角对比损失拉近不同视角下同类特征；(4) 自适应增强策略，自动分类输入场景类型并路由到最优增强，同时DynamicScaleRouter学习逐输入特征金字塔权重。

Result: 在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP，推理时间为2.91ms（T4 GPU）。在鱼眼基准上提升+4.1 mAP、全景基准+6.6 mAP、无人机基准+6.4 mAP、游戏角色基准+26.1 mAP。

Conclusion: YOLOv14通过统一框架有效解决了实时目标检测器在非理想输入下的性能退化问题，在保持实时推理效率的同时，在多个特殊场景基准上均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Real-time object detectors achieve remarkable accuracy under controlled conditions, yet degrade sharply on non-ideal inputs: fisheye distortion, game-rendered characters, aerial viewpoints, and 360° panoramas. We present YOLOv14, aunified detection framework addressing these challenges through four synergisticinnovations. (1) Deformable Area-Attention (D-AAttn) replaces rigid attentiongrids with learned 2D deformation fields, enabling adaptive sampling under geometric distortion. (2) Game2Real Domain Adaptation aligns rendered-game and photographic feature distributions via Adaptive Instance Normalization (AdaIN)and adversarial domain confusion, allowing game characters are detected as realhumans. (3) Multi-View Conditioning injects learned viewpoint embeddings intothe backbone with a cross-view contrastive loss that pulls same-class features fromdifferent perspectives closer. (4) An Adaptive Augmentation Policy automaticallyclassifies each input' scene type and routes to optimal augmentations, while a DynamicScaleRouter learns per-input feature pyramid weights. Together, YOLOv14achieves 49.1 mAP on COCO val2017 at 2.91 ms (T4 GPU), and delivers substantial gains on fisheye (+4.1 mAP), panorama (+6.6 mAP), drone (+6.4 mAP), andgame-character (+26.1 mAP) benchmarks

</details>


### [74] [Multi-View Face and Gesture Animation with Dynamic Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04722)
*Alireza Javanmardi,Vippin Kumar Jeetmal,Christen Millerdurai,Alain Pagani,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D human avatars with realistic upper-body motion remains challenging. Existing approaches either focus on the head and overlook hand gestures, or reconstruct the full body but fail to preserve fine-grained facial fidelity and hand pose accuracy. As a result, current methods struggle to capture the subtle dynamics of facial expressions and hand gestures that are crucial for natural human communication. While methods based on full-body parametric models enable avatar reconstruction from monocular or multi-view inputs, they often lack accurate facial animation and detailed hand articulation. To address these limitations, we propose MVFGA, a novel multi-view-consistent pipeline for generating realistic upper-body avatars. Our approach models the face and hands separately and fuses them with a parametric upper-body mesh model, enabling the capture of fine-grained facial expressions and hand poses for accurate upper-body avatar reconstruction. We then splat 3D Gaussians onto the obtained mesh, enabling high-quality rendering of dynamic avatars from novel viewpoints. Furthermore, we introduce MVFGA-MoCap, a multi-view upper-body motion capture dataset featuring controlled facial expression sequences, diverse hand gestures, and free-form communication. Experiments show that MVFGA generates visually realistic avatars with high-fidelity facial expressions and hand motions, outperforming baselines for upper-body avatar animation. Project page: https://dfki-av.github.io/MVFGA/

</details>


### [75] [Dense Metric Depth Completion from Sparse Direct Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04737)
*Hakyeong Kim,Ruicheng Wang,Chengtang Yao,Jiaolong Yang,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于稀疏直接飞行时间（dToF）传感器的密集度量深度补全框架，采用深度引导的双分支ViT编码器和掩码联合注意力模块，配合全面的dToF仿真管线，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现强零样本泛化，且在准确性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dToF传感器虽然能提供高精度度量深度，但因其制造成本高、光电二极管阵列尺寸有限，产生的深度图极其稀疏、低分辨率且含噪，无法满足VR/XR、机器人等需要密集度量深度的3D感知任务。现有的单目和深度补全方法无法应对dToF设备独特的采样模式和硬件伪影，在严重稀疏或噪声条件下性能显著下降。

Method: 1）提出深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，分别处理RGB图像和稀疏dToF测量；2）引入掩码联合注意力模块，使深度token可靠地引导图像特征而不被其覆盖；3）采用轻量级解码器高效重建密集度量深度，无需扩散或细化后处理；4）构建全面的dToF仿真管线，复现闪光、sub-VGA闪光和旋转传感器的特性，包括硬件退化、不规则稀疏性和真实噪声分布。

Result: 模型完全在合成数据上训练，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现强零样本泛化，在准确性和计算效率两方面均超越最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本研究为基于稀疏dToF传感器的密集度量深度补全提供了一种鲁棒且实用的解决方案，验证了合成训练数据配合精心设计的架构能够有效泛化到多种真实传感器类型、稀疏程度和噪声条件，树立了该领域的实用基线。

Abstract: Direct Time-of-Flight (dToF) sensors provide highly accurate metric depth and are more robust than indirect ToF systems in challenging real-world conditions. However, their high manufacturing cost and limited photodiode array size produce depth maps that are extremely sparse, low-resolution, and noisy, making them unsuitable for VR/XR, robotics, and 3D perception tasks that require dense metric depth. Existing monocular and depth completion methods struggle to handle the unique sampling patterns and hardware artifacts of dToF devices, and their performance often deteriorates significantly under severe sparsity or noise. We present a generalizable framework for dense metric depth completion from sparse dToF measurements, capable of operating across diverse sensor types, sparsity levels, and noise conditions. Our model employs a depth-guided dual-branch Vision Transformer encoder that processes RGB images and sparse dToF measurements separately, while a masked joint attention module allows depth tokens to reliably guide image features without being overwritten by them. A lightweight decoder reconstructs dense metric depth efficiently, without diffusion-based or refinement-heavy post-processing. To address the scarcity of paired training data, we introduce a comprehensive dToF simulation pipeline that reproduces the characteristics of flash, sub-VGA flash, and rotating sensors, including hardware-induced degradation, irregular sparsity, and realistic noise distributions. Trained entirely on synthetic data, our model achieves strong zero-shot generalization across 6 datasets and 3 real dToF devices, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches in both accuracy and computational efficiency. This establishes a robust and practical solution for dense metric depth completion from sparse direct ToF sensors. Our code and models are open-sourced. See https://vclab.kaist.ac.kr/cvpr2026p3.

</details>


### [76] [Simile Understanding in Text-to-Image Models: An Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04750)
*Luecheng Wang,Shintaro Ozaki,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Jingun Kwon,Manabu Okumura,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可扩展的评估框架来研究文本到图像模型（t2i）对明喻（simile）的理解能力，发现前沿模型普遍存在"字面化"失败——将喻体误当作本体。该框架包括受控明喻数据集、基于YOLO的自动接地（grounding）指标，以及基于Diffusion Lens的文本编码器层分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明喻以紧凑且富有表现力的方式描述视觉特征，但现有的t2i模型即使用前沿模型也经常误解明喻中的喻体（metaphorical vehicle），将其与本体（object）混淆。这种系统性的失败揭示了t2i模型中比喻语言与对象级视觉接地之间的鸿沟，但目前缺乏针对该问题的可扩展评估方法。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：（1）受控明喻数据集——喻体从预定义的对象可检测类别中抽取，并结合多样化模板生成；（2）基于YOLO检测的自动接地指标；（3）利用Diffusion Lens进行文本编码器层分析，追踪明喻生成过程中的喻体涌现机制。

Result: 在多种架构风格的t2i模型上进行的实验显示出一致的"字面化"失败模式，即模型倾向于将喻体按字面意义理解而非其隐喻含义。

Conclusion: 该研究系统揭示了t2i模型在明喻理解上的系统性缺陷，并讨论了潜在缓解策略以改进t2i模型中的明喻接地能力。

Abstract: Similes provide a compact and expressive way to describe visual characteristics in text prompts. Recent text-to-image models (t2i models) can produce visually compelling outputs from simile prompts, yet even frontier models frequently misinterpret the metaphorical vehicle and confuse it with the object. These systematic failures reveal a gap between figurative language and object-level visual grounding in t2i models. To investigate this issue, we propose a scalable evaluation framework for simile understanding. Our framework includes (1) a controlled simile dataset in which metaphorical vehicles are drawn from a predefined set of object-detectable categories and combined with diverse templates, (2) automatic grounding metrics based on YOLO (You Only Look Once) detection, and (3) text encoder layer analysis using Diffusion Lens to track how metaphorical vehicles emerge during generation. Experiments across architecturally diverse t2i models reveal consistent literalization failure patterns. We further discuss potential mitigation strategies for improving simile grounding in t2i models.

</details>


### [77] [Revisiting Pose Sensitivity in Splat-based Computed Tomography under Sparse-view Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04752)
*Kiseok Choi,Hyeongjun Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出基于3D高斯splatting的锥束稀疏视角CT在实际系统中因采集几何姿态不准确而产生严重的条状和条带伪影，并提出一种轻量级的梯度优化框架，在重建过程中联合精化几何参数，从而显著提升真实稀疏视角条件下的重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管splat-based断层重建在锥束稀疏视角CT中展现了高质量和快速收敛的优势，但在真实CT系统中面临有限且非均匀的视角分布时，会产生比传统重建方法更明显的条纹和条带伪影。作者通过分析发现这些伪影主要源于采集几何中的姿态不精确而非视角稀疏本身，因此需要研究姿态敏感性并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者首先深入分析了姿态扰动如何通过可微投影算子传播，揭示了splat-based CT对几何错位特别脆弱的机理。基于这一分析，重新审视了splatting公式中的姿态敏感性，推导出一个稳定的基于梯度的框架，在重建过程中联合优化几何参数。

Result: 该公式保持轻量化，易于集成到现有重建管线中，在真实世界稀疏视角条件下显著提高了重建保真度，同时仅带来极小的额外成本。

Conclusion: splat-based CT在真实环境中的伪影主要由几何姿态误差驱动而非视角稀疏性，通过联合精化几何参数的稳定梯度框架可有效解决该问题，为实际CT系统中的高保真稀疏视角重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) reconstructs volumetric representations of objects from projection images obtained by transmitting X-rays through a target. Recent splat-based tomography, which represents a volume as a continuous distribution of 3D Gaussians, has demonstrated both high reconstruction quality and fast convergence in cone-beam sparse-view CT. However, when deployed in real CT systems with limited and non-uniform view distributions, we observe distinctive streak and strip artifacts that are far more pronounced than in conventional reconstruction methods. Through detailed analysis, we show that these artifacts primarily originate from pose inaccuracies in the acquisition geometry rather than from view sparsity itself. We revisit pose sensitivity in the splatting formulation and derive a stable gradient-based framework that jointly refines geometric parameters during reconstruction. Our study not only identifies how pose perturbations propagate through the differentiable projection operator but also reveals why splat-based CT is particularly vulnerable to geometric misalignment. The resulting formulation remains lightweight and easily integrable into existing pipelines while substantially improving reconstruction fidelity under real-world sparse-view conditions.

</details>


### [78] [Trace, Verify, and Correct: A Training-Free Framework for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04759)
*Yang Yang,Jiawei Chen,Tairan Chen,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化、无需训练的框架，通过构建空间证据图（SEG）和空间证据可靠性评估（SERA）来验证和纠正多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在空间推理中的思维链（CoT），确保推理过程忠实于输入图像，在15个模型-数据集组合上平均准确率达68.94%，比基线高出8.55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLM在空间推理上已有显著进展，但其推理过程中间判断可能与输入图像不一致，错误会沿推理链传播并影响最终答案。现有方法主要通过训练或额外空间信息提升空间推理能力，未考虑推理过程本身是否忠实于模型输入。研究显示，不忠实的推理链会显著降低最终答案的准确率。

Method: 提出模块化且无需训练的框架：(1) 构建空间证据图（SEG），将CoT推理中提取的原子空间证据与视觉实体、空间关系、源步骤和视觉证据关联；(2) 通过空间证据可靠性评估（SERA）基于对象存在性、定位和几何测量评估视觉证据的可靠性；(3) 识别被可靠视觉证据反驳的最早空间证据单元，引导原始MLLM修正后续推理和最终答案。

Result: 在15个模型-数据集组合上进行评估，方法平均准确率达68.94%，较对比基线平均高出8.55个百分点。

Conclusion: 验证了不忠实的空间推理链会损害MLLM最终答案的准确性，并证明通过系统化的空间证据验证与纠正机制，无需训练即可有效提升MLLM的空间推理可靠性。代码将开源。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress, their spatial reasoning may still produce intermediate judgments inconsistent with the input image, allowing errors to propagate through the reasoning chain and affect the final answer. Existing methods mainly improve spatial reasoning through training or additional spatial information, without considering whether the reasoning process itself is faithful to the model input. Our study shows that unfaithful reasoning chains significantly reduce final-answer accuracy. To address this issue, we propose a modular and training-free framework for spatial reasoning verification and correction. The framework constructs a Spatial Evidence Graph (SEG), which associates atomic spatial evidence extracted from Chain-of-Thought reasoning with visual entities, spatial relations, source steps, and visual evidence. Spatial Evidence Reliability Assessment (SERA) evaluates the reliability of visual evidence based on object existence, localization, and geometric measurements. The framework then identifies the earliest spatial evidence unit contradicted by reliable visual evidence and guides the original MLLM to revise the subsequent reasoning and final answer. Across 15 model-dataset settings, our method achieves an average accuracy of 68.94%, outperforming the compared baselines by 8.55 percentage points on average. Our code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [79] [Splat-Based Metal Artifact Reduction in Cone-Beam CT via Compact Attenuation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04764)
*Kiseok Choi,Jaemin Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯溅射（splat-based）的金属伪影消除框架，将物理驱动的多色前向模型集成到连续高斯表示中，用于锥束CT重建，无需金属掩膜即可联合优化几何和材料属性，在模拟和真实数据上比现有方法更快收敛且更有效地抑制金属伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥束CT在存在高衰减物体（如牙科填充物或骨科植入物）时会产生严重金属伪影，源于X射线的多色特性打破传统重建算法的单色假设。现有神经渲染方法尝试通过可微多色投影模型解决该问题，但仍存在平滑性偏差、细结构丢失以及扩展到大规模锥束CT时计算代价过高的问题。

Method: 提出一种溅射式金属伪影消除框架，将物理驱动的多色前向模型集成到连续高斯表示中。每个高斯通过紧凑的材料参数化编码底层材料的能量依赖衰减特性，无需依赖金属掩膜即可高效地联合优化几何属性和材料属性。该紧凑衰减公式能够捕捉生物组织和金属植入物之间的关键变化，在解释金属诱导的非线性效应的同时保留高频结构。

Result: 在模拟和真实锥束CT扫描上的实验表明，该方法比现有重建方法和基于神经场的方法收敛速度显著更快，且能更有效地抑制金属伪影。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于高斯的溅射框架通过物理驱动建模有效解决了锥束CT中的金属伪影问题，在效率和重建质量上均优于现有方法，为大规模CT金属伪影消除提供了可行的新途径。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) suffers from severe metal artifacts when high-attenuation objects such as dental fillings or orthopedic implants are present. These artifacts originate from the polychromatic nature of X-rays, where attenuation varies strongly with photon energy and material composition, breaking the monochromatic assumption used by conventional reconstruction algorithms. Recent neural rendering approaches attempt to address this mismatch through differentiable polychromatic projection models, but they still struggle with smoothness bias, loss of fine structures, and prohibitive computation when extended to large-scale cone-beam CT. We introduce a splat-based metal artifact reduction framework that incorporates a physically grounded polychromatic forward model into a continuous Gaussian representation for cone-beam CT. Each Gaussian encodes the energy-dependent attenuation of the underlying material using a compact material parameterization, which enables efficient joint optimization of geometric and material properties without relying on a metal mask. This compact attenuation formulation captures the essential variation across biological tissues and metallic implants, allowing our model to explain metal-induced nonlinearity while preserving high-frequency structure. Experiments on simulated and real cone-beam CT scans show that our method converges significantly faster and suppresses metal artifacts more effectively than existing reconstruction and neural field-based approaches.

</details>


### [80] [Embedding Large Language Models into Flow Controls: An Agentic Framework for Adaptive and Trustworthy Automated Cooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04768)
*Zihan Song,Hongwei Huang,Yueshuo Sun,Yonglin Tian,Fei-Yue Wang,Bai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代理（agentic）框架，将个性化烹饪需求系统分解为结构化、可验证的控制程序，通过多AI代理协作完成从自然语言意图到可执行代码的转化，并采用离线生成、在线闭环执行、事后适应三阶段架构，在真实物理烹饪平台上验证了可靠的任务完成、透明的执行逻辑和有效的异常处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化烹饪机器人依赖预定义程序和规则控制，稳定性好但个性化能力有限；而近期大模型方法虽支持自然语言交互，却存在决策不透明和真实厨房环境中执行不可靠的问题。亟需一个兼顾个性化、可靠性和可解释性的解决方案。

Method: 提出三阶段紧密耦合的代理框架：(1) 离线阶段通过多个AI代理协作将用户意图转化为标准食谱、包含显式流程控制的工作流程序，以及基于原子动作库的可执行Python代码；(2) 在线阶段实现闭环执行，通过多模态感知实现监督干预；(3) 事后阶段更新用户偏好模型以支持长期个性化。

Result: 在真实物理烹饪平台上的实验表明，该框架在多样化个性化场景中实现了可靠的任务完成、透明的执行逻辑和有效的异常处理，验证了其在真实环境中可信自动化烹饪的实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将语言到动作的直接映射转变为结构化程序生成，兼顾了个性化与可验证性，为可信赖的自动化烹饪系统提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Automated cooking robots have traditionally relied on predefined procedures and rule-based control, ensuring stable execution but offering limited personalization, whereas recent large-model approaches support natural language interaction but often suffer from opaque decision making and unreliable execution in real kitchens. To address this challenge, this paper proposes an agentic framework that systematically decomposes personalized cooking requirements into structured and verifiable control programs rather than directly mapping language to actions. Multiple AI agents collaboratively transform user intents into canonical recipes, workflow programs with explicit flow control, and executable Python code grounded in an atomic action library. The system consists of three tightly coupled stages: offline recipe-to-code generation through multiple agents, online closed-loop execution with supervisory intervention enabled by multimodal perception, and post-run adaptation that updates user preference models for long-term personalization. Real-world experiments on a physical cooking platform demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves reliable task completion, transparent execution logic, and effective anomaly handling across diverse personalized scenarios, validating its practicality for trustworthy automated cooking in real environments.

</details>


### [81] [On the Effectiveness of Adaptation Strategies for VLM-Based Federated Learning in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04791)
*Simon Lösche,Barış Büyüktaş,Mathis Adler,Angelos Zavras,Ioannis Papoutsis,Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了联邦学习（FL）中不同视觉-语言模型（VLM）适配策略在遥感图像分类中的表现，涵盖全量微调、编码器微调、提示学习和LoRA微调，并从非IID数据下的泛化能力、通信开销和本地计算复杂度三个维度进行了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中非IID数据分布会阻碍模型收敛并限制全局模型的泛化能力。VLM具有可迁移的表示能力，但参数规模巨大，在联邦设置下会显著增加通信开销和本地计算复杂度。因此需要选择一种合适的VLM适配策略，以在泛化能力与通信/计算约束之间取得平衡。

Method: 本文首次对联邦学习中遥感图像分类的VLM适配策略进行比较研究，考察了四种适配方法：全量微调、编码器特定微调、提示学习和低秩适配（LoRA）微调。评估基于三个标准：1）非IID数据下的泛化能力；2）通信开销；3）本地计算复杂度。实验在BigEarthNet-S2、EuroSAT、RESISC45和ImageNet四个数据集上进行。

Result: 实验揭示了任务特化、跨域泛化与效率之间不同的权衡关系。不同适配策略在非IID数据下的泛化能力、通信开销和计算复杂度上各有优劣。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者提出了一套指导准则，帮助在不同操作约束下为联邦学习的遥感图像分类选择合适的VLM适配策略。代码已公开。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of deep learning models across decentralized image archives without requiring data centralization. This paradigm is particularly relevant in remote sensing (RS), where legal regulations, privacy concerns, and bandwidth constraints restrict data sharing. However, the presence of training data heterogeneity across clients (known as non-IID data) can impede convergence and limit the generalization capability of the aggregated global model. To mitigate the adverse effects of training data heterogeneity, vision-language models (VLMs) can be leveraged in FL due to their transferable representations, which have demonstrated robustness under distribution shifts. However, their large parameter size may substantially increase communication overhead and local computational complexity in federated settings. Therefore, it is crucial to select an appropriate VLM adaptation strategy that balances the generalization ability with the communication and computational constraints. To address this issue, in this paper, we present the first comparative study of VLM adaptation strategies for FL in the context of RS image classification. We investigate full fine-tuning, encoder-specific fine-tuning, prompt learning, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) tuning, and analyze them with respect to three criteria: 1) generalization capability under non-IID data, 2) communication overhead, and 3) local computational complexity. Experiments on BigEarthNet-S2, EuroSAT, RESISC45, and ImageNet reveal distinct trade-offs between task specialization, cross-domain generalization, and efficiency. Based on our findings, we derive a guideline for the selection of an appropriate VLM adaptation strategy in FL for RS image classification under different operational constraints. The code of this work is publicly available at https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/FL-RS-VLM.

</details>


### [82] [Segmentation Pre-training for Label-Efficient Lumbar Spine Degeneration Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04810)
*Monzon Maria,Zisserman Andrew,Jutzeler Catherine R.,Jamaludin Amir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在腰椎MRI退行性病变分级任务中，能否用分割预训练替代部分人工分级标注。通过3D ResNet编码器在椎骨、椎间盘和椎管分割上预训练（Dice 0.94），再微调轻量级分级头，在约2000名受试者、11种病理的多中心数据集上，分割预训练在所有标注比例下均提升了宏平均ROC-AUC，仅用20%分级标签即可接近全监督性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎MRI退行性病变的自动化分级评估需要大规模专家标注的放射学分级数据集，而收集这些标注成本高昂。相反，分割伪标签可以以极低的放射科医生成本通过自动化工具生成。作者希望探索利用分割预训练来减少对人工分级标注的依赖，从而降低标注成本。

Method: 预训练一个3D ResNet编码器用于分割椎骨、椎间盘和脊髓管（获得Dice 0.94的伪标签分割性能），然后在该编码器上微调轻量级的任务特定分级头。实验使用10%到100%不同比例的训练数据进行微调，在包含约2000名受试者、11种病理的多中心数据集上评估性能。

Result: 分割预训练在所有数据比例下均提升了任务平均（宏）one-vs-rest ROC-AUC。仅使用20%的分级标签配合预训练，即可达到接近全监督的性能。最大性能增益出现在低患病率或空间定位明确的（空间相关的）病理上。

Conclusion: 分割预训练可以有效地替代一部分人工分级标注需求，显著降低数据标注成本，同时保持接近全监督的性能水平。该方法对低患病率及空间相关病理尤其有效。

Abstract: Automated assessment of degenerative pathology in the lumbar spine on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) requires access to large-scale datasets of expert-annotated radiological gradings. In contrast, segmentation pseudo-labels can be generated by automated tools at negligible radiologist cost. We examine whether pre-training on segmentation can effectively replace a fraction of the manual grading annotations required for downstream supervision. We pre-train a 3D ResNet encoder to segment the vertebrae, intervertebral discs (IVDs), and the spinal canal, then fine-tune lightweight task-specific grading heads using different proportions of the available training data, ranging from $10\%$ to $100\%$. On a multicentre dataset of ${\sim}2{,}000$ subjects across 11 pathologies, segmentation pre-training, achieving a Dice score of $0.94$ against pseudo-labels, improved the task-averaged (macro) one-vs-rest ROC-AUC at all proportions. With only 20\% of grading labels after pre-training, the method achieved near full-supervision performance, with the largest gains observed for either low-prevalence or spatially grounded pathologies.

</details>


### [83] [StaticSegFormer: An Efficient High-Performance Semantic Segmentation Based on Static Structured Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04811)
*Timo Bartels,Danish Nazir,Jan Piewek,Thorsten Bagdonat,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StaticSegFormer，一种针对语义分割网络中注意力层的静态结构化剪枝方法，在SegFormer网络上同时实现更低的FLOPs和更高的帧率（Cityscapes上fps提升高达34%），且不损失mIoU性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态结构化剪枝方法虽然在mIoU和FLOPs上与静态方法相当，但在GPU平台上帧率低下（远低于简单的静态方法）。为解决这一性能瓶颈，需要设计一种既能降低FLOPs又能保持高帧率的静态剪枝方法。

Method: 提出StaticSegFormer，一种针对注意力层的静态结构化剪枝方法。与动态方法不同，它在不增加运行时开销的前提下剪除注意力层中的参数组，从而同时获得低FLOPs和高帧率。

Result: 在ADE20K和Cityscapes基准上，StaticSegFormer在Cityscapes数据集上使SegFormer的帧率相对提升高达34%，FLOPs更低，且mIoU性能无任何下降。该方法在小型编码器和大尺寸图像场景下表现最强。

Conclusion: 静态剪枝可以作为动态剪枝的有效替代方案，在保持mIoU的同时同时优化FLOPs和推理帧率，尤其适用于小编码器和大图像的语义分割场景。

Abstract: Structured pruning enhances the efficiency of deep neural networks (DNNs) by eliminating groups of parameters during inference. Previous methods mostly reduce computational complexity (FLOPs), while semantic segmentation performance (mIoU) slightly drops. Accordingly, recent dynamic structured pruning methods aim at reducing the performance drop, while lowering the FLOPs even more. However, on the ADE20K and Cityscapes benchmarks, our study reveals that on a GPU platform such dynamic methods exhibit a surprisingly low frame rate far below a simple static approach, while having comparable results in mIoU and FLOPs. To address this issue, we propose a static structured pruning method for attention layers, that achieves both, a lower FLOPs and a high frame rate [fps] of the SegFormer network, the latter increased by up to 34% relative on the Cityscapes dataset, while having no mIoU performance drop at all. Our so-called StaticSegFormer method is strongest for small encoders and large images.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking Pixel Mean Flows via Interval Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04818)
*Alexander Zaytsev,Dmitry Baranchuk,Alexander Korotin,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Interval Denoiser，一种无需外部自编码器的潜在自由生成框架，直接从流匹配ODE推导出中间轨迹状态的精确解析映射，使网络可直接在像素上回归，并在ImageNet 256×256上以1步FID 4.55、2步FID 3.98实现最优生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代扩散和基于流的模型正向少步、无潜在变量生成方向发展，以避免多步采样的计算开销和外部自编码器带来的重建瓶颈。然而现有方法在潜在自由设定下回归中间状态存在可求解性差、梯度有偏等问题。

Method: 1) 直接从流匹配ODE推导出中间轨迹状态的精确解析映射，建立Interval Denoiser框架；2) 证明预测驻留在低维流形上，使像素级回归可处理；3) 避免经验代数替换，正确隔离纯时间导数以保证梯度无偏和精确一阶优化；4) 引入残差裁剪和时间采样课程，实现长间隔训练并提升少步性能。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上从头训练，无需感知损失，单步（1-NFE）达到FID 4.55，两步（2-NFE）达到FID 3.98。

Conclusion: Interval Denoiser通过理论严谨的推导和训练策略优化，在潜在自由的少步生成中取得了显著性能提升，验证了其框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern diffusion and flow-based models are increasingly moving toward few-step, latent-free generation to bypass the computational overhead of multi-step sampling and the reconstruction bottlenecks of external autoencoders. We propose the Interval Denoiser, a theoretically rigorous framework for latent-free generation. Derived directly from the flow matching ODE, it establishes an exact analytical mapping for intermediate trajectory states. Unlike prior formulations, our prediction is shown to reside on a low-dimensional manifold across any time interval, making the regression tractable for a network operating directly on pixels. Furthermore, by avoiding empirical algebraic substitutions, our formulation correctly isolates the pure time derivative to prevent biased gradient evaluations and ensure exact first-order optimization. By analyzing this objective, we equip our framework with residual clipping and a time-sampling curriculum, enabling effective long-interval training and improving few-step performance. Trained from scratch on ImageNet 256x256, our model achieves an FID of 4.55 in one step (1-NFE) and 3.98 in two steps (2-NFE) without perceptual losses.

</details>


### [85] [When Diffusion Models Forget Who You Are: Identity Preservation in Face Inpainting under Large Occlusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04820)
*Feng Ding,Shuhuai Xie,Yue Zhou,Yulan Zhang,Guopu Zhu,Mengyao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReSem-Face是一个级联扩散框架，通过引入显式的身份条件语义先验来进行多参考人脸修复，在严重遮挡和矛盾文本引导下保持身份保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于扩散模型的人脸修复已取得令人印象深刻的视觉质量，但在严重遮挡和冲突文本引导下保持身份保真度仍是一大挑战。

Method: 提出ReSem-Face级联扩散框架：从多个参考图像中提取代表性身份特征以重建缺失的语义区域，并通过多流条件架构将这些语义先验引导扩散过程，在像素缺失时提供强语义约束，同时保持与提示驱动编辑的兼容性。

Result: 在CelebAHQ-IDI-5和VGGFace2数据集上的实验表明，ReSem-Face在严重语义掩膜下实现了更可靠的身份保持完成效果，并相比代表性基线方法提升了文本控制编辑质量。

Conclusion: 通过显式身份条件语义先验和多流条件架构，ReSem-Face在严重遮挡场景下有效平衡了身份保真与文本驱动编辑之间的兼容性，验证了语义先验在扩散修复框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Face inpainting with diffusion models has recently achieved impressive visual quality, yet preserving identity fidelity under significant occlusion and conflicting text guidance remains a major challenge. To address this issue, we present Reference Semantic Inpainting for Face (ReSem-Face), a cascaded diffusion framework that introduces an explicit identity-conditioned semantic prior for multi-reference face inpainting. Our approach distills representative identity features from multiple references to reconstruct missing semantic regions, which then guide the diffusion process through a multi-stream conditioning architecture. This design provides strong semantic constraints when pixels are absent and stabilizes identity reconstruction while remaining compatible with prompt-driven edits. Experiments on CelebAHQ-IDI-5 and VGGFace2 demonstrate that ReSem-Face yields more reliable identity-preserving completion under severe semantic masks and improves text-controlled editing quality compared with representative baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Global Attention-Fused Image Cropping with Attention-Guided and Global-Aligned Crop Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04821)
*Haotian Yang,Zhile Yang,Kin-Man Lam,Patrick Le Callet,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全局注意力融合图像裁剪方法（GAFIC），通过注意力引导特征融合和全局对齐裁剪评估器，在保持像素完整性的前提下选择美学上更优的裁剪区域，在GAIC和CPC数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像裁剪方法主要关注显著性区域，对图像主要组件之间的全局关系敏感度有限，无法充分理解图像的整体结构，导致裁剪结果在美学上不够理想。

Method: GAFIC包含两个核心模块：Attention-Guided Feature Fusion (AGFF)聚合局部区域重要性以构建同时捕捉图像结构和局部细节的全局表示；Global-Aligned Crop Evaluator (GACE)将候选裁剪特征与全局表示对齐，使裁剪评估对边界变化保持敏感。此外，结合多个尺度的三种排序损失以获得准确且稳定的裁剪分数。

Result: 在GAIC和CPC数据集上的大量实验表明，GAFIC在准确性和稳定性方面均优于现有图像裁剪方法。不同于像素级重定向方法（如接缝裁剪、修复和基于扩散的合成），GAFIC不合成或修改保留像素，而是从源图像中选择美学上更优的裁剪，适合需要像素完整性和高效批量处理的场景。

Conclusion: GAFIC通过全局注意力融合机制有效解决了现有方法对图像全局关系感知不足的问题，提供了更准确、更稳定的图像裁剪性能，同时保持了像素完整性。

Abstract: Image cropping aims to improve image aesthetics by preserving important content within an appropriately composed region. However, most existing methods focus primarily on salient regions and therefore have limited sensitivity to the global relationships among the main image components. To address this limitation, we propose Global Attention-Fused Image Cropping (GAFIC), which consists of an Attention-Guided Feature Fusion (AGFF) and a Global-Aligned Crop Evaluator (GACE). AGFF aggregates the importance of local regions to construct a global representation that captures both image structure and local details. GACE aligns candidate crop features with this global representation, enabling crop evaluation to remain sensitive to boundary changes. We further combine three ranking losses across multiple scales to obtain accurate and stable crop scores. Extensive experiments on the GAIC and CPC datasets demonstrate that GAFIC outperforms existing image-cropping methods, particularly in terms of accuracy and stability. Unlike pixel-level retargeting methods such as seam carving, inpainting, and diffusion-based synthesis, GAFIC does not synthesize or modify the retained pixels; instead, it selects an aesthetically preferred crop from the source image, making it suitable for scenarios where pixel integrity and efficient batch processing are important. The source code is available at https://github.com/AIVRC/GAFIC.git.

</details>


### [87] [RegisterBridgeMM: A Register-Centric Framework for RGB-Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04833)
*Zian Wang,Hangchuan Liang,Yuehua Chen,Changchun Li,Chaoyi Guo,Mingzhe Liu,Fangming Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RegisterBridgeMM，一种基于注册令牌（register token）中介的RGB-红外目标检测融合框架，通过三阶段注册生命周期（聚合-桥接-投影）实现跨模态通信，避免密集的patch级交互，在五个基准上达到最优mAP50-95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-IR融合方法常在表达性的patch级交互与轻量但受限的适应机制之间做权衡，难以在光照变化、天气变化和杂乱场景下有效融合。作者观察到预训练注册令牌在配对RGB-IR输入上同时包含模态共享与模态特定信息，可作为紧凑的跨模态通信载体。

Method: 提出RegisterBridgeMM，采用三阶段注册生命周期：（1）Aggregate阶段保留预训练中每模态的注册令牌摘要；（2）Bridge阶段执行双向注册令牌到patch的读取，并通过共识-残差调节（consensus-residual regulation）实现跨模态通信；（3）Project阶段将注册摘要转化为空间自适应的patch特征校准。整个流程避免密集的patch-to-patch跨模态交互，同时保持预训练patch表征。

Result: 在两个骨干流完全冻结的条件下，RegisterBridgeMM在LLVIP、M3FD、DroneVehicle和FLIR-Aligned四个基准上均达到所有评估方法中的最高mAP50-95。

Conclusion: 注册令牌可作为有效的紧凑跨模态通信媒介，使融合在避免密集补丁交互的同时保持高精度，且在骨干网络冻结的情况下仍能取得最优性能，证明了该方法的高效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: RGB-infrared (RGB-IR) object detection benefits from complementary visible and thermal cues, but effective fusion remains challenging under illumination changes, weather variation, and cluttered scenes. Existing RGB-IR fusion methods often trade expressive patch-level interaction for lighter but more constrained adaptation mechanisms. We empirically observe that pretrained register tokens contain both modality-shared and modality-specific information on paired RGB-IR inputs, suggesting that they can serve as a compact substrate for cross-modal communication. Building on this observation, we propose RegisterBridgeMM, a register-mediated fusion framework organized as a three-stage register lifecycle. Aggregate preserves per-modality register summarization inherited from pretraining; Bridge performs bidirectional register-to-patch reading with consensus-residual regulation; and Project translates the resulting register summary into spatially adaptive calibration of patch features. This register pathway avoids dense patch-to-patch cross-modal interaction while preserving the pretrained patch representation. With both backbone streams frozen, RegisterBridgeMM achieves the highest mAP50-95 among the evaluated methods on all four benchmarks: LLVIP, M3FD, DroneVehicle, and FLIR-Aligned.

</details>


### [88] [Cooking beyond Frames: A Stereo Event Camera Dataset in the Kitchen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04865)
*Chengming Feng,Hesam Araghi,Liming Zheng,Julien Dupeyroux,Xucong Zhang,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tömen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EventKitchen，一个大规模立体事件相机基准数据集，记录了10名参与者在13个不同厨房中自然执行烹饪活动（无脚本动作）的5.5小时数据，包含同步的RGB、深度和IMU数据，以及10,762个动作片段和13,482个边界框的人工标注，用于事件相机在动作识别、目标检测和立体深度估计等任务上的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机研究多集中于自动驾驶和无人机应用，人类日常生活中的场景（如厨房烹饪活动）代表性不足。此外，现有的事件相机人类活动数据集通常使用脚本化的动作录制，无法捕捉自然的人类行为，限制了对事件感知系统的开发和基准测试。

Method: EventKitchen以自我中心视角（egocentric）采集，10名参与者在13个不同厨房中佩戴带有多个传感器的头盔自然执行烹饪活动（无任何脚本化动作），记录5.5小时的立体事件数据，并同步采集RGB、深度和IMU数据。数据包含10,762个动作片段和13,482个边界框的人工标注。作者在数据集上训练基线模型执行多种事件感知任务，包括动作识别、目标检测和立体深度估计。

Result: EventKitchen建立了超越自动驾驶领域的神经形态视觉挑战性基准，通过捕捉自然、真实世界的人类活动，为事件相机在人类日常生活场景中的多种任务（动作识别、目标检测、立体深度估计）提供了评测平台。

Conclusion: EventKitchen填补了事件相机数据集中人类日常场景的空白，通过非脚本化、自然录制的多模态厨房活动数据，为神经形态视觉研究提供了一个更具挑战性和真实性的基准数据集，推动了事件感知系统从自动驾驶向更广泛的人类中心场景的扩展。

Abstract: Event cameras, also known as neuromorphic cameras, have gained significant attention in recent years due to their high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and low power consumption. While many studies and datasets in neuromorphic vision have focused on automotive and drone applications, human-centric daily-life scenarios remain largely underrepresented, despite their importance for developing and benchmarking event-based perception systems. Moreover, the few existing event-based human activity datasets are typically recorded with scripted human actions, limiting their ability to capture natural human behaviors. In this paper, we introduce EventKitchen, a large-scale stereo event camera benchmark dataset of human cooking activities in the kitchen. EventKitchen is egocentrically collected from 10 participants in 13 diverse kitchens, where the participants wear a helmet with multiple sensors and naturally perform cooking activities, without any scripted actions. EventKitchen comprises 5.5 hours of stereo event recordings with synchronized RGB, depth, and IMU data. We provide human annotations for 10,762 action segments and 13,482 bounding boxes. We train baseline models on EventKitchen to perform multiple event-based tasks, including action recognition, object detection, and stereo depth estimation. By capturing natural, real-world human activities, EventKitchen establishes a challenging benchmark for neuromorphic vision beyond autonomous driving.

</details>


### [89] [Persistent Object Narratives for Token-Efficient Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04866)
*Junzhe Chen,Siyuan Meng,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotNarrative，一种基于槽（slot）的视频大语言模型（Video-LLM）视觉接口，通过将视频组织为持久化的对象叙事（以紧凑的对象状态令牌表示），利用轻量级、无需参数的内存机制整合多种互补匹配线索来关联跨时间的对象证据，最终仅使用144个视觉令牌位置即可驱动冻结的Video-LLM，在多个数据集上取得了准确率与视觉令牌数量之间的有利权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLM的视觉接口令牌密集且缺乏显式结构来跨时间链接重复出现的对象证据，导致视频理解效率低且时间对应关系不清晰。

Method: SlotNarrative方法分为两步：首先将视觉特征分组为对象类槽（object-like slots），然后通过轻量级、无需参数的记忆机制，整合多种互补匹配线索（如外观、几何等），将重复出现的观测与片段级对象条目关联。每个保留条目被序列化为两类令牌：身份令牌（概括持久对象外观）和状态令牌（编码片段级外观、几何、可见性和轨迹信息）。整个接口仅分配144个视觉令牌位置，与采样帧数无关。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SlotNarrative相比先前紧凑型Video-LLM接口，在准确率与视觉令牌数量之间取得了更有利的权衡，验证了持久化对象叙事作为紧凑、结构化、时间组织的视觉接口的有效性。

Conclusion: 持久化对象叙事是一种紧凑、结构化且时间上有组织的视觉接口，能够高效地为Video-LLM提供视频理解所需的视觉信息。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have made strong progress in open-ended video understanding. However, their visual interfaces remain token-intensive and provide limited explicit structure for linking recurring object evidence across time. We introduce SlotNarrative, a slot-based interface that organizes a video into persistent object narratives represented by compact object-state tokens. Rather than compressing frame-wise features before establishing temporal correspondence, SlotNarrative first groups visual features into object-like slots and then associates recurring observations with clip-level object entries through a lightweight, parameter-free memory that integrates multiple complementary matching cues. Each retained entry is serialized into two token types: an identity token that summarizes persistent object appearance and a set of state tokens that encode segment-level appearance, geometry, visibility, and trajectory information. This design yields an interface of only 144 allocated visual-token positions for a frozen Video-LLM, independent of the number of sampled frames. Across multiple datasets, SlotNarrative achieves a favorable trade-off between accuracy and visual-token count compared with prior compact Video-LLM interfaces. Experimental results establish persistent object narratives as a compact, structured, and temporally organized visual interface for Video-LLMs. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [90] [Evaluating the Diagnostic Robustness of Vision-Language Models Under Visual and Textual Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04885)
*Ali Khoramfar,Mohammad Javad Dousti,Alireza Mohamadian,Heshaam Faili*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统地评估了四种VLM（视觉语言模型）在脑MRI诊断任务中的鲁棒性，发现模型在保持临床证据不变的扰动下（如切片重排、标签位置交换）会出现高达48.9%的预测翻转和67.8%的标签选择偏差，并在去病灶阴性对照中产生76.1%的过度诊断，表明高准确率掩盖了临床可靠性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标常掩盖VLM在敏感领域中的重大可靠性失败，需要更细粒度的稳定性评估来揭示模型在实际临床部署中的真实风险。

Method: 使用组织病理学验证的脑MRI数据集，设计三类证据保持扰动实验：(1) 重排解剖切片顺序；(2) 交换目标标签位置；(3) 移除专家标注的病灶切片进行阴性对照。评估四个VLM家族在上述条件下的预测一致性。

Result: 研究发现：序列反转导致最多48.9%的预测翻转；标签重排引发67.8%的不一致诊断；去除病灶切片后模型仍给出76.1%的明确分类诊断。三种扰动均揭示高准确率下的稳定性缺陷。

Conclusion: 高准确率并不能代表临床可靠性，VLM对序列呈现顺序和文本框架的敏感性是聚合准确率无法捕捉的；安全性关键的临床应用需要引入基于稳定性的评估指标。

Abstract: Standard accuracy metrics for VLMs often mask significant reliability failures in sensitive domains. In this work, we utilize a histopathology-validated brain MRI dataset to systematically assess the diagnostic robustness of four VLM families under evidence-preserving perturbations. By reordering anatomical slices and swapping target label positions, we evaluate whether models maintain consistent predictions when clinical evidence remains invariant. Our results reveal significant vulnerabilities in presentation-order stability, with models exhibiting prediction flips in up to 48.9% of cases under simple sequence reversals. We further identify a textual selection bias, where label reordering triggers inconsistent diagnoses in up to 67.8% of cases despite identical visual inputs. Negative-control tests further reveal diagnostic overcommitment: models generate categorical diagnoses in up to 76.1% of cases after expert-annotated lesion slices are removed. These results demonstrate that high accuracy can overestimate clinical reliability, masking sensitivity to sequential presentation and textual framing that is not captured by aggregate accuracy. Our findings highlight the necessity of stability-based metrics for the deployment of VLMs in safety-critical clinical applications. Our evaluation data and code will be made public upon acceptance.

</details>


### [91] [STEP-OPD: Rethinking Output Targets and Internal Dynamics in On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04887)
*Qingyan Wei,Guangzhao Li,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Xiaohong Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STEP-OPD是一个面向图像生成的在线蒸馏（OPD）框架，通过引入输出外推（output extrapolation）和块级表示演化对齐（blockwise representation change alignment），使统一学生模型超越教师模型的能力上限，在组合对齐、文本渲染和人类偏好等多个任务上均显著优于标准OPD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法主要让学生匹配教师的输出速度（velocity），这使教师成为优化目标的上限。仅靠输出级别的监督会使学生在各网络块之间的内部表示演化约束不足（underconstrained），削弱了需要跨层逐步发展的能力迁移效果。

Method: STEP-OPD包含两个核心策略：(1) 输出外推：将每个任务特定教师与共享基础模型之间的速度差作为进一步学习的方向，并将该差值的缩放版本添加到教师速度上，使学生学习目标超越教师本身；(2) 块级表示演化对齐：对齐学生和教师之间表示变化的方向和幅度，让学生学习表示如何在网络各块之间渐进式变换。

Result: 在组合对齐、文本渲染和人类偏好三个能力维度的实验中，STEP-OPD一致性地提升了标准OPD方法的表现。具体而言，将DiffusionOPD的GenEval分数从0.927提升到0.961，同时改进了OCR和所有基于偏好的指标。统一学生模型在全部三个能力组上超过了对应的单任务教师。

Conclusion: 输出外推使统一学生能够实现超越教师的学习，而块级表示演化对齐则为学生的内部变换提供了互补的指导信号。两者结合有效克服了传统OPD中学生受限于教师能力上限的问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an effective approach for consolidating multiple task-specialized image generation models into a single student. However, existing OPD methods optimize the student mainly to match the teacher's output velocity, making the teacher the upper limit of the optimization objective. While output-level supervision alone leaves the student's blockwise representation evolution underconstrained, which weakens the transfer of capabilities that must be progressively developed across layers. We propose STEP-OPD, an on-policy distillation framework for image generation that extends the student's learning target beyond the teacher and introduces explicit constraints on its internal representation evolution. Instead of treating the teacher as the final target, we use the velocity difference between each task-specific teacher and the shared base model as a direction for further learning and add a scaled version of this difference to the teacher velocity. In addition, we align the direction and magnitude of representation changes between the student and teacher, enabling the student to learn how representations are progressively transformed across network blocks. Experiments on compositional alignment, text rendering, and human preference show that our method consistently improves Standard OPD methods. In particular, it increases the GenEval score of DiffusionOPD from 0.927 to 0.961, while also improving OCR and all preference-based metrics. The resulting unified student surpasses the corresponding single-task teachers across all three capability groups, showing that output extrapolation enables beyond-teacher learning. And representation change alignment provides complementary guidance for the student's internal transformations.

</details>


### [92] [Visual Representation Matters: Exploiting Temporal Differences in Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04902)
*Zehua Chen,Junyou Wang,Yuxuan Jiang,Zhenying Fang,Yusheng Dai,Jianfei Chen,Ziwei Liu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-V2A，利用时间差分（TD）作为视频到音频（V2A）生成的核心表征，以最小化架构改动增强视觉条件，通过层级连续学习策略和退火时间差分引导方法，在扩散训练和采样过程中逐步学习并利用TD信息，显著提升端到端V2A生成质量，甚至超越对比音频-视觉预训练等专用V2A表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于条件扩散的V2A方法通常需要额外的音视频监督、声学结构预测或大模型推理来增强视觉条件，这些方法依赖额外网络或强归纳偏置，架构复杂且效率低。作者希望寻找一种简洁、有效的表征来区分V2A与I2A，以最小架构改动提升生成质量。

Method: 1) 从帧级和特征级两个层面研究时间差分（TD）表示，确定TD在哪个层面能最有效地补充视觉表征；2) 基于发现设计层级连续学习（hierarchically continual learning）策略；3) 提出退火时间差分引导（annealed temporal differences guidance）方法，在扩散训练和采样过程中逐步学习和利用TD信息。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，有效利用TD可显著改善端到端V2A生成质量，性能甚至优于对比音频-视觉预训练（contrastive audio-visual pretraining）等专用V2A表示方法。

Conclusion: 时间差分是区分V2A与I2A的关键表征，以极小的架构改动即可有效增强视觉条件，为V2A生成提供了一种简洁且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation extends image-to-audio generation (I2A) by introducing consecutive frames that provide essential temporal cues for audio synthesis. However, existing conditional diffusion-based V2A methods typically enhance visual conditioning with additional audio-visual supervision, acoustic structure prediction, or reasoning from large multimodal models, requiring extra networks or strong inductive biases. Inspired by recent advances in visual representation learning, we introduce TD-V2A, which leverages temporal differences (TD) as the key representation that distinguishes V2A from I2A, enriching visual conditioning with minimal architectural modification. We first investigate TD at both the frame and feature levels to identify the most effective representation level at which TD complements visual representations. Based on these findings, we develop a hierarchically continual learning strategy and an annealed temporal differences guidance method to progressively learn and exploit TD information during diffusion training and sampling process, respectively. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that effectively exploiting TD through our proposed framework significantly improves end-to-end V2A generation quality, even outperforming dedicated V2A representations such as contrastive audio-visual pretraining.

</details>


### [93] [Enhancing Low Back Pain Assessment with Diffusion Models for Lumbar Spine MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04906)
*Maria Monzon,Thomas Iff,Ender Konukoglu,Catherine R. Jutzeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpineSegDiff，一个基于扩散模型的腰椎MRI语义分割框架，适用于T1和T2加权像，在SPIDER数据集上与先进非扩散模型nnUnet对比，达到了可比的性能，尤其在退化椎间盘识别上表现更优，并能为临床审查提供不确定性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎MRI分割对下背痛（LBP）的诊断与管理至关重要，但现有方法对病理状态下的腰椎结构（尤其退化椎间盘）分割的鲁棒性和准确性不足，且缺乏不确定性估计来支持临床决策。

Method: 采用扩散模型（diffusion-based framework）构建分割框架SpineSegDiff，对腰椎MRI中的椎体、椎间盘和椎管进行语义分割，同时生成不确定性图。模型能够同时处理T1和T2加权MRI扫描，并在SPIDER数据集上与包括nnUnet在内的先进模型进行系统比较。

Result: SpineSegDiff在分割性能上与当前最先进的非扩散模型nnUnet相当，尤其在退化椎间盘识别方面有所提升。同时，模型生成的不确定性图为临床审查提供了有价值的参考，增强了分割结果的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明扩散模型在提高病理脊椎MRI分析精度方面具有潜力，能够为下背痛的诊断和管理提供更准确的影像学支持。

Abstract: This study introduces a diffusion-based framework for robust and accurate semantic segmentation of lumbar spine MRI scans from patients with low back pain (LBP), regardless of whether the scans are T1- or T2-weighted. We compared with advanced models for segmenting vertebrae, intervertebral discs (IVDs), and spinal canal using the SPIDER dataset. The results showed that SpineSegDiff achieved a segmentation performance comparable to that of the state-of-the-art non-diffusion nnUnet, particularly in improving the identification of degenerated IVDs. In addition, the uncertainty maps generated by our model provide valuable insights for clinical review, enhancing the robustness and reliability of the segmentation results. The potential of diffusion models to enhance the diagnosis and management of LBP through more precise analysis of pathological spine MRI is underscored by our findings.

</details>


### [94] [An active-learning framework for real-time depth perception from monocular vision streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04917)
*Xiaorong Zeng,Weiqiang Chen,Peng Shi,Liang Su,Zirui Wang,Xuewu Ji,Shuiwen Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在线主动学习机制，使轻量级神经网络能够在部署后持续适应动态环境中的单目深度估计任务，通过预测-评估-纠正的闭环学习范式和弹性权重整合（EWC）实现选择性可塑性，在MobileNetV3-Small骨干网络上实现约75%的计算成本降低，同时保持有竞争力的深度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上部署的轻量级模型通常以静态离线方式训练并在部署后冻结，在域偏移下性能严重退化。虽然大型模型可通过参数冗余编码广泛知识，但轻量网络面临静态优化困境——强制紧凑模型学习通用几何表示计算效率低且容易性能饱和。因此需要一种机制使紧凑模型在运行过程中持续适应。

Method: 提出在线主动学习（OAL）机制，建立预测-评估-纠正的闭环学习范式，从流式视觉输入中主动选择高置信度、信息丰富的信号。核心创新是将弹性权重整合（EWC）从单纯防止灾难性遗忘的工具，升级为强制执行"选择性可塑性"的手段——保留编码全局相关结构知识的参数，同时允许对新增环境的局部对齐。系统基于MobileNetV3-Small骨干网络构建。

Result: 在MobileNetV3-Small骨干上实现约75%的计算成本降低，同时保持有竞争力的深度估计精度。实验结果揭示：模型的适应性并非仅由模型大小决定，而是取决于在动态环境中如何有效调控参数可塑性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，轻量级神经网络的持续适应能力可以通过巧妙的参数可塑性调控来实现，而非单纯依赖模型规模。在线主动学习与选择性可塑性相结合，为边缘智能设备在动态环境中的可靠深度感知提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Biological visual systems can perceive depth from monocular vision flow, continuously integrating temporal visual cues while maintaining a balance between stability and plasticity in dynamic environments. In contrast, artificial perception models deployed on resource-constrained edge devices are typically trained in a static offline manner and remain frozen after deployment, often suffering severe performance degradation under domain shifts. While large-scale models may encode broad knowledge through massive parameter redundancy, lightweight networks face a static optimization dilemma: forcing compact models to learn universal geometric representations is computationally inefficient and often leads to performance saturation. To resolve this issue, an Online Active Learning (OAL) mechanism is introduced to endow compact neural networks with the capability to adapt continuously during operation. A closed-loop Predict-Evaluate-Correct learning paradigm is established to actively select high-confidence, information-rich signals from streaming visual input. Crucially, Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is employed not merely to prevent catastrophic forgetting, but to enforce Selective Plasticity, preserving parameters that encode globally relevant structural knowledge while allowing local alignment to newly observed environments. Built upon a MobileNetV3-Small backbone, the proposed system achieves approximately a 75% reduction in computational cost while maintaining competitive depth estimation accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that adaptability is not solely determined by model size, but rather by how effectively parameter plasticity is regulated in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [95] [Unleashing the Potential of Vision-Language Models for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04935)
*Weihan Cai,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Jun Wan,Xinping Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型的感知编码器（PE）在AIGI检测中比DINOv3具有更强的潜在能力，并提出语义原型校准（SPC）方法，通过构造类别原型并结合监督数据校准，使PE-SPC检测器在跨生成器、后处理和野外场景基准上超越DINOv3基线，达到新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证实DINOv3是AIGI检测的主导基线，但作者发现基于简单线性探针的方法未能充分利用表示空间中的高阶层级语义结构，因此探索是否具有更强能力的基础模型表示可用于进一步提升AIGI检测性能。

Method: 提出语义原型校准（SPC）方法：首先从取证语义信息中构造类别原型，然后使用监督数据进行校准，并将其应用于感知编码器（PE）的冻结表示上，形成PE-SPC检测器。

Result: PE-SPC在跨生成器、后处理和野外基准上超越了DINOv3基线，实现了新的最先进检测性能，验证了语言对齐表示和语义原型校准在AIGI检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言对齐的表示保留了丰富的来源语义信息且具有更好的局部来源组织能力，配合语义原型校准这一简单设计即可显著提升AIGI检测的泛化能力，为后续AIGI检测研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that a simple linear probe on frozen representations from modern vision foundation models (VFMs) can achieve state-of-the-art AIGI detection performance, substantially outperforming specialized detectors in challenging in-the-wild scenarios. This finding has established DINOv3 as the dominant foundation-model baseline for subsequent improvements. However, we find that the vision-language model Perception Encoder (PE) holds greater potential for AIGI detection, because its language-aligned representation preserves high-level provenance semantics. Specifically, PE exhibits stronger local provenance organization than DINOv3 in its frozen feature space. However, semantic-agnostic linear probing fails to exploit this structure, as PE-Linear still underperforms DINOv3-Linear by 4.1% on In-the-Wild. Based on this observation, we propose Semantic Prototype Calibration (SPC), which constructs category prototypes from forensic semantic information and calibrates them with supervised data. We apply SPC to PE and refer to the resulting detector as PE-SPC. Our analysis shows that this simple design achieves stronger generalization. Across cross-generator, post-processing, and in-the-wild benchmarks, PE-SPC surpasses the previous DINOv3 baseline and achieves new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [96] [UG-UMRE: Uncertainty-Guided Modality Augmentation and Distributional Calibration for Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04949)
*Bo Kong,Liruiz Jia,Yi Liang,Chao Liu,Dongfang Han,Tianwei Yan,Yuan Liu,Shengquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UG-UMRE网络用于统一多模态关系抽取，通过不确定性驱动的单模态增强模块和联合任意不确定性对齐模块，分别解决噪声传播和模态分布异质性问题，在三个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMRE研究存在两大关键问题：1) 忽略固有的任意不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）导致噪声传播；2) 不同模态分布之间的深层异质性阻碍了对齐。

Method: 1) UDUA模块：基于变分信息瓶颈将特征建模为高斯分布，结合不确定性感知的自监督对比学习机制，在保持语义一致性的同时过滤噪声。2) JAUA模块：作为全局语义预校准机制，利用概率分布一致性构建共享潜空间，通过同步跨模态统计属性消除分布差异，为细粒度交互奠定基础。

Result: 在UMRE、MORE和MNRE三个基准数据集上取得最先进性能；消融分析验证了UDUA和JAUA模块的可插拔性和有效性。

Conclusion: UG-UMRE通过显式建模不确定性成功解决了UMRE任务中的噪声传播和模态异质性问题，且所提出的模块具有良好的可移植性。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction (UMRE) aims to identify intra-modal and cross-modal relations between textual entities and visual objects. However, existing UMRE studies still encounter two critical issues: ignoring inherent aleatoric uncertainty causes noise propagation, and deep-seated heterogeneity between distinct modal distributions hinders alignment. To address these issues, we propose the Uncertainty-Guided UMRE Network (UG-UMRE). Specifically, we design an Uncertainty-Driven Unimodal Augmentation (UDUA) module, which models features as Gaussian distributions based on the Variational Information Bottleneck. By incorporating an uncertainty-aware self-supervised contrastive learning mechanism, UDUA effectively filters out noise while maintaining semantic consistency. Furthermore, we introduce the Joint Aleatoric Uncertainty Alignment (JAUA) module as a global semantic pre-calibration mechanism. JAUA leverages probabilistic distribution consistency to construct a shared latent space, eliminating the distributional gap by synchronizing cross-modal statistical properties, thereby laying a robust foundation for fine-grained interaction. Experiments on three benchmark datasets (UMRE, MORE, and MNRE) demonstrate that UG-UMRE achieves state-of-the-art performance. Further analysis validates the pluggable and effective performance of the proposed UDUA and JAUA modules.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Valid B-Rep Generation: Training-Free Wireframe Anomaly Detection and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04955)
*Jingyu Wu,Youcheng Cai,Tengyu Luo,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WDR是一个无需训练的框架，在B-Rep生成的中间线框阶段进行干预，通过几何-拓扑异常检测器和能量引导修复模块，提升下游CAD模型的有效性，可无缝集成到自回归和扩散管线中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段B-Rep生成依赖中间线框合成CAD模型，但线框中的几何和拓扑缺陷（如自交、边塌缩、顶点断开）会传播到最终B-Rep导致无效结果。通过重训大型生成模型来缓解此类失败在计算上不可行，因此需要一种无需训练的干预方案。

Method: WDR包含两大核心模块：(1) 几何-拓扑异常检测器(GTAD)，结合并行VLM粗筛和几何/拓扑检测器预测下游无效风险并路由到专用修复分支；(2) 能量引导几何-拓扑修复(EGGTR)模块，通过几何和拓扑分支执行检测器触发的引导重生成。利用能量引导重采样和扩散模型的无需训练引导来扩展测试时计算。

Result: 大量实验表明WDR在核验证有效性(kernel-checked validity)上有一致提升，同时基本保持了所合成CAD模型的多样性和分布质量。

Conclusion: WDR证明了通过测试时干预而非重训练即可显著提升B-Rep生成的有效性，为大型生成模型的可靠性问题提供了一条低成本、可插拔的解决路径。

Abstract: Multi-stage boundary representation (B-Rep) generation leverages intermediate wireframes to synthesize CAD models. However, geometric and topological risks in these wireframes -- such as self-intersections, edge collapses, and disconnected vertices -- can propagate to invalid final B-Reps. Mitigating such failures by retraining large generative models is computationally prohibitive. We propose Wireframe Detection and Repair (WDR), a training-free framework that intervenes at the intermediate wireframe stage to improve downstream B-Rep validity. WDR features a Geometric-Topology Anomaly Detector (GTAD) that combines parallel VLM-based coarse screening with geometric and topological detectors to predict downstream invalidity risk and route generation to dedicated branches. An Energy-Guided Geometric-Topology Repair (EGGTR) module then performs detector-triggered guided regeneration through geometry and topology branches. By scaling test-time computation via Energy-Guided Resampling and training-free guidance for diffusion models, WDR can be integrated into autoregressive and diffusion pipelines without retraining. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in kernel-checked validity while largely retaining the measured diversity and distributional quality of synthesized CAD models. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [98] [ContextMaster: Interactive Multi-Shot Video Creation via Fixed-Budget Sparse Context Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04956)
*Xu Guo,Zhengxuan Wei,Xinghui Li,Hanzhuo Huang,Xinyu Liu,Xiangyang Luo,Min Wei,Yiran Zhu,Qiulin Wang,Yulong Xu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Qi Fan,Xiangwang Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextMaster，一个统一的多镜头视频创作模型，通过角色感知的上下文表示和稀疏上下文路由，在固定预算下支持文本生成、参考条件化和视频编辑等多种操作，并通过两阶段特权上下文蒸馏框架在少步推理下实现高效部署，达到单GPU 16 FPS的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型虽然支持生成、参考条件化和编辑，但将它们视为对固定输入的独立操作。实际创作涉及多个镜头，需要单一模型能够从文本生成、跟随参考或编辑源素材，同时维护共享历史。然而，交互式模型必须访问不断扩展的历史，但上下文读取成本不能随去噪步数增长，这构成了核心挑战。

Method: ContextMaster包含三个关键组件：(1) 角色感知上下文表示，统一处理文本生成、参考条件化和视频编辑三类操作；(2) 可复用的干净上下文状态与固定预算的稀疏上下文路由，控制上下文读取成本；ConstraintSink保持任务约束可见；(3) 两阶段特权上下文蒸馏框架——先从密集教师模型通过一致性蒸馏转移完整上下文行为，再用分布匹配细化部署时的rollout，以应对稀疏上下文访问和少去噪步数的双重挑战。

Result: 在三种基本任务上的实验表明，ContextMaster相较于专门化的基线模型，在任务完成度和跨镜头一致性方面均有提升。用户研究验证了灵活组合工作流的有效性，模型在单GPU上达到16 FPS的推理速度。

Conclusion: ContextMaster成功地将文本生成、参考条件化和视频编辑统一到单一交互式多镜头视频创作框架中，在保持高效推理速度的同时实现了更好的任务完成度和跨镜头一致性，为实际视频创作中的多操作组合工作流提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent video models increasingly support generation, reference conditioning, and editing within a single model, yet typically expose them as separate operations over fixed inputs. Practical creation unfolds across multiple shots, requiring one model to generate from text, follow a reference, or edit source footage while maintaining shared history. We formalize this setting as interactive multi-shot video creation (IMVC) and introduce ContextMaster, a unified model with a role-aware context representation for these operations. An interactive model must retain access to an expanding history without allowing the context read cost at each denoising step to grow. ContextMaster combines reusable clean context states with fixed budget sparse context routing and uses ConstraintSink to keep task constraints visible. To address the dual challenges of sparse context access and inference with few denoising steps, we propose a two-stage privileged context distillation framework, which transfers full context behavior from a dense teacher through consistency distillation and then refines deployment rollouts with distribution matching. Experiments on the three primitive tasks demonstrate improved task fulfillment and consistency across shots over specialized baselines. User studies further validate flexibly composed workflows, while the model reaches 16 FPS on a single GPU.

</details>


### [99] [Promptable Animal Pose Tracking Across Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04995)
*Le Li,Daniela Ivanova,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用视觉基础模型进行动物姿态估计与跟踪的框架，包含监督和无监督两种模型，在有限标注数据下实现了精度与泛化能力的平衡，并在APTv2和TigDog基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物姿态估计与跟踪对野生动物监测和保护研究至关重要，但注释数据有限且专家标注时间稀缺。现有方法要么依赖标注数据集（如APTv2）但泛化能力差，要么使用视觉跟踪定制关键点但性能受限。需要一种在少量标注数据下既能保持高精度又具备跨物种泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出两种模型：(1) 监督方法：利用关键点提示编码器（keypoint prompt encoder）从参考帧显式注入结构先验到特征匹配中，提升跟踪精度；(2) 无监督方法：利用多样化的基础模型特征进行无需训练的对应匹配，提供强跨物种鲁棒性。两种方法均基于在大规模数据集上预训练的视觉基础模型。

Result: 在挑战性动物视频基准APTv2和TigDog上的大量评估表明，该框架取得了强性能，同时在精度和泛化能力之间保持了有效平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为真实世界动物行为分析和保护应用提供了实用解决方案，证明了视觉基础模型在有限标注条件下可有效用于动物姿态跟踪。

Abstract: Animal pose estimation and tracking is important for wildlife monitoring and conservation research, and with limited expert time for labelling automated approaches are imperative. While human pose estimation and tracking has seen rapid progress thanks to large annotated datasets, animal pose remain challenging, due to large morphological and behavioural differences between species and limited annotated data. Existing approaches either optimise generic keypoint localisation from annotated datasets (such as APTv2) with poor generalisation, or track custom keypoints using visual tracking, at the cost of performance. In this paper, we demonstrate that vision foundation models trained on large datasets can be used effectively to track animal pose with limited labelled data. We propose two models, one unsupervised and the other supervised, to track user-selected keypoints in videos. The supervised approach delivers superior tracking accuracy by employing a keypoint prompt encoder to explicitly inject structural priors from a reference frame into feature matching. In parallel, the unsupervised route provides strong cross-species robustness by leveraging diverse foundation-model features for training-free correspondence matching. Extensive evaluation on challenging animal video benchmarks APTv2 and TigDog demonstrates that our framework achieves strong performance while maintaining an effective balance between accuracy and generalisation, offering a practical solution for real-world animal behaviour analysis and conservation applications.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Physics of Multimodal Pretraining: Knowledge Flow, Modality Synergy, Early Unification, and Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05000)
*Junlin Han,Shengbang Tong,David Fan,Minghao Chen,Philip Torr,Filippos Kokkinos,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性受控实验（涵盖合成数据和大规模真实数据）探究多模态预训练中模态交互的内在机制，提出四大核心洞察：知识流动的差异性与不对称性、数据复杂度决定模态协同与竞争的机制、早期统一训练优于晚期对齐（并发现"视觉懒惰"现象）、以及仅需5%计算预算的高效训练配方。最终通过多个13.5B MoE模型在2T token上的训练验证了这些发现的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉正推动基础模型向原生统一多模态预训练方向发展，但模态在统一训练中如何交互的设计空间和基本机制仍未被充分探索。作者旨在通过系统实验为这一核心问题提供经验性的清晰认识。
